HARBOR BROOK AT SYRACUSE NY, USGS site 04240100 (USGS-04240100), stream in Onondaga County, New York, draining 10 square miles. Daily means from 1959-06-19 to 2020-09-30, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
